## Group: product

### checkBookingEligibility

**`POST /eligibility-checks`**: Can this party take part

Checks a party's declared ages and heights against every product in the booking before payment, and says per guest who cannot take part and why, and whether a guardian signature or waiver is needed. **It refuses nothing itself**: checkout refuses a line with an ineligible guest, so the answer here and the refusal there cannot disagree.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `catalogue.product`, `catalogue.product_eligibility_rule` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | GST-007, WEB-006 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**: `EligibilityCheckRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| productIds | array of string | yes | (min items 1) |

### getProductEligibilityRule

**`GET /products/{productId}/eligibility-rule`**: Who may take part: age, height, supervision

**A product with no rule set answers `200`, not `404`.** The schema's own reading of absence is "anyone may take part", so an unset rule comes back with every limit null and every other field at its default — the body a rule that limits nothing would return. `404` means the product itself is unknown or outside the caller's scope.

### listProducts

**`GET /products`**: List products

For guest, partner and back-office callers. **A point-of-sale terminal does not call this on the server** — on a till it is answered from the local catalogue bundle (`getLatestBundle`, ADR-0013), which is what `x-ticvai-offline-capable` means here. A till screen that lists this operation is reading the bundle.
**What a guest caller gets** (decided 29 September, rev 3 REV3-14). Products with `guestListing: bookable` that are on sale at the caller's channel, plus products with `guestListing: infoOnly` whose lifecycle is live, whether or not they are on sale, each carrying its `guestListing` and `notBookableLabel` so the screen shows the label and opens details instead of adding to the basket. `hidden` products are never returned to a guest, and `infoOnly` ones are left out when the venue's `BookingFlowConfig.showInfoOnly` is off. Staff and partner callers see every product, whatever its `guestListing`.
**Category and tag filters** (decided 29 September, rev 3 REV3-16 and REV3-19). `categoryId` narrows to one category of the `listProductCategories` tree, so a guest can go from a category tile to that category's tickets; `segmentTag` narrows by tag, e.g. a surf level.
**Help me choose filters the list** (decided 29 September, W4). `guidedAnswerIds` applies the filter of each chosen answer on the server; the guest screen offers *Show everything*, which is the same call without it. **A partner caller reads here** what is assigned to its channel (M17-04): partners never write products, prices or performances.
**A guest calls this without a permission and sees published data only** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-001; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest or a visitor gets products that are published and on sale, and nothing in draft, unpublished or withdrawn; asked for by id, such a record answers as not found. `PRODUCT_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold, and staff also see drafts (ADR-0025).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Guest callable | True |
| Offline note | 24 August: servable from a local cache. |
| Reads | `catalogue.product`, `catalogue.product_category`, `catalogue.product_eligibility_rule`, `catalogue.product_media`, `whitelabel.guided_choice_answer` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | ANL-004, BO-007, BO-012, BO-013, BO-014, BO-048, BO-094, BO-125, BO-877, CMS-101, CMS-103, EMP-034, GST-001, GST-002, GST-003, GST-005, GST-007, GST-008, GST-015, GST-021, GST-038, GST-044, GST-050, GST-051, GST-052, GST-053, GST-075, KSK-001, KSK-003, KSK-015, POS-002, POS-003, PTR-006, WEB-001, WEB-002, WEB-003, WEB-005, WEB-022, WEB-035, WEB-048, WEB-050 |

### setAlternativeCodes

**`PUT /products/{productId}/alternative-codes`**: Set external identifiers

**What the PUT does.** The body is the product's whole set of alternative codes: a code left out is removed, a code sent is stored as sent, and an empty `codes` removes them all. A code is unique per partner. Every `variantId` must be a variant of this product; one that belongs to another product is a `400`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `catalogue.alternative_code` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.alternative_code` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `catalogue.alternative_code` |
| Called by | BO-007, BO-012 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| productId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| codes | array of AlternativeCode | yes |  |
| codes[].code | string | yes | (max length 128) |
| codes[].partnerId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| codes[].partnerName | string |  |  |
| codes[].variantId | string (uuid) |  |  |
| codes[].note | string |  | (max length 200) |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Set |
| 400 |  | A variantId is not a variant of this product, or the body sends one code twice for the same partner. |
| 409 |  | Code already mapped to a different product for that partner |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

### setProductEligibilityRule

**`PUT /products/{productId}/eligibility-rule`**: Set age, height and supervision limits

**Design gap, 23 September.** The design gates rides, slides and kids' activities on height and age before booking (*Height & age eligibility gate*, *Swim ability & height gate*). The catalogue had no place for the limits; only games, queues and rentals did.

**Declared, then checked at the gate.** The guest declares a band at booking; staff verify on arrival. Whether an ineligible guest is refunded at the gate is a setting, because the design says they are not.

### updateProductVariant

**`PATCH /products/{productId}/variants/{variantId}`**: Describe a variant

**Only the words and codes a person reads or scans.** A variant's combination of values (`axisValues`) and its `sku` come from `setProductAttributes` and are not edited here; this sets the `name`, the `description` a guest reads behind the (i) on a ticket-type row (decided 29 September, 23SEP-6), the `barcode` and which variant is the default.
Setting `isDefault` true clears it on the product's other variants. A retired variant is not edited: `409`.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, changes rows of `catalogue.variant` that another operation creates |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `catalogue.variant` |
| Writes | `catalogue.variant` |
| Called by | BO-008 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| productId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| variantId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| name | string |  | (max length 150; nullable) |
| description | object |  | (nullable) |
| barcode | string |  | (max length 64; nullable) |
| isDefault | boolean |  |  |

**Response**: `ProductVariant`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| id | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| productId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| sku | string | yes |  |
| axisValues | object | yes |  |
| name | string |  | Taken from their variant tables, 20 September. (max length 150; nullable) |
| barcode | string |  | Taken from their variant tables, 20 September. (max length 64; nullable) |
| isDefault | boolean |  | Taken from their variant tables. (default False) |
| isActive | boolean | yes | False when retired. |
| description | object |  | Who this ticket type is for and what it includes, shown behind the (i) on each Adult, Child, Senior or Infant row (decided 29 September, 23SEP-6). (nullable) |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Updated. |

## Group: promotion

### analysePromotionConflicts

**`GET /promotions/{promotionId}/conflicts`**: Analyse stacking against live promotions

Run before publishing. Reports promotions that overlap on product and period, and what the combined discount would be.
**"Near-zero" has a stated minimum** (decided 28 September, audit R096 (5)): a combination is near-zero when the net line price it leaves falls below the venue setting `promotions.nearZeroLinePrice` (a tenant default the venue may override; proposed AED 1.00, client to correct under audit R094). It is flagged with `isNearZero` for a person to look at. Only zero or below blocks publication (`isBlocking`, audit R101).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Reads | `promotions.promotion` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | BO-010, POS-002 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |

| conflicts | array of object | yes |  |
| conflicts[].otherPromotionId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| conflicts[].otherPromotionCode | string | yes |  |
| conflicts[].overlap | enum (products, period, channel, full) | yes |  |
| conflicts[].combinedDiscount | number | yes | Combined percentage where both apply to the same line. |
| conflicts[].isBlocking | boolean |  | True where the combination would produce a line price of zero or below (decided 28 September, audit R101). |
| conflicts[].isNearZero | boolean |  | True where the combination leaves a net line price above zero but below the venue setting promotions.nearZeroLinePrice (proposed AED 1.00; decided 28 September, audit R096 (5)). |
| worstCaseDiscount | number | yes | Largest combined discount any single line could receive. |

|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Analysis |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

### createPromotion

**`POST /promotions`**: Create a promotion

Created in `draft`. A draft promotion never evaluates — publishing is the act that makes it live, and it appears at terminals with the next catalogue bundle.
**Writes `promotions.promotion` and `promotions.promotion_audit`** in the same transaction, as every promotions configuration change does (entityType `promotion`, eventType `promotionCreated`, promotionVersion 1). No channel publication row exists until publishPromotion (decided 29 September, writers pass).

| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit` |

### getPromotion

**`GET /promotions/{promotionId}`**: Read a promotion

**A guest calls this without a permission and sees published data only** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-001; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest or a visitor gets a live, published promotion, and nothing in draft, unpublished or withdrawn; asked for by id, such a record answers as not found. `PRICE_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold, and staff also see drafts (ADR-0025).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | yes |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Read routing | replica |
| Guest callable | True |
| Offline note | 24 August: servable from a local cache. |
| Reads | `promotions.promotion` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | ADM-140, BO-010, GST-037, POS-002, PTR-010, WEB-032 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Promotion. |
| 404 | NotFound | The resource does not exist, or is outside the caller's scope. |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

### getPromotionUsage

**`GET /promotions/{promotionId}/usage`**: Redemption count and discount given

Also the enforcement surface for budget caps — a promotion may be configured to stop automatically once a total discount value is reached.
**A budget cap is refused at checkout, not only reported** (decided 28 September, audit R101): once `discountGiven` plus the discount on the order being checked out would exceed `budgetCap`, the promotion no longer applies to that order and `isBudgetExhausted` is true.

| Writes | - |
| Called by | ADM-138, ADM-140, BO-010, POS-002, PTR-010 |

### publishPromotion

**`POST /promotions/{promotionId}/publish`**: Publish a promotion

Runs conflict analysis first. A promotion that stacks with an existing one to produce a negative or near-zero line price is refused — that combination is discovered at a till otherwise, usually by a guest.
**The floor is zero: a combined line price of zero or below is refused** (decided 28 September, audit R101). A line that stays above zero, however small, publishes. One that falls below the near-zero minimum (`ConflictAnalysis`, audit R096 (5)) publishes too, flagged rather than refused.
**Writes `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` and `promotions.promotion_audit`.** One row per sales channel the promotion runs on goes to `pendingPublication` (created there when the channel has none, resumed from `suspended` when it has), for the channel sync job to take up (states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml); the audit row is eventType `promotionActivated` with the published promotionVersion. The conflicts found here are recorded in `promotions.promotion_conflict` by the conflict detection job, not by this operation (decided 29 September, writers pass).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `promotions.promotion` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `platform.outbox`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Called by | ADM-141, BO-010 |
| State model | Promotion channel publication ([states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml](../../../states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml)): moves `notAssigned` -> `pendingPublication`, `suspended` -> `pendingPublication`<br/>Promotion ([states/promotion.yaml](../../../states/promotion.yaml)): moves `draft` -> `scheduled`, `paused` -> `live` |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Response**: `Promotion`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| code | string | yes | Unique per tenant (6 October 2026, CHG-R4-015; the rule of audit R108 for configuration codes). (max length 64; pattern ^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$) |
| name | string | yes | (max length 200) |
| description | string |  | (max length 1000) |
| venueId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| discount | Discount | yes |  |
| discount.kind | DiscountKind: enum (percentage, fixedAmount, fixedPrice, buyXGetY, freeItem, tieredPercentage) | yes |  |

### updatePromotion

**`PATCH /promotions/{promotionId}`**: Amend a promotion

Amending a live promotion changes behaviour mid-sale. Conditions and discount are editable only in draft; a live promotion may be paused, extended or ended, nothing more.
**Writes `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` and `promotions.promotion_audit`.** Each channel row of the promotion that is `published` moves to `outOfSync` (states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml) and the sync job pushes the new `version`; the audit row records the changed fields before and after (eventType `promotionEdited`, or `discountChanged`, `eligibilityChanged`, `datesChanged` where only that changed) with the new promotionVersion (decided 29 September, writers pass).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, changes rows of `promotions.promotion` that another operation creates |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit`, `promotions.promotion_channel_publication` |
| Called by | BO-010 |
| State model | Promotion channel publication ([states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml](../../../states/promotion-channel-publication.yaml)): moves `published` -> `outOfSync` |

## Group: promotions

### setPromotionVariants

**`PUT /promotions/{promotionId}/variants`**: A/B test two versions against each other

BL-114. **Split by a stable hash of the subject, not at random per request** — a guest who sees 10% on Monday and 15% on Tuesday has learned to wait.
**The `trafficPercent` of all variants must sum to exactly 100; there is no minimum per variant** (decided 28 September, audit R101). A variant at 0 is kept but receives no traffic.
**Writes `promotions.promotion_variant` and `promotions.promotion_audit`** in the same transaction, as every promotions configuration change does (entityType `promotion`, eventType `promotionEdited`) (decided 29 September, writers pass).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRICE_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `promotions.promotion` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_variant` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `promotions.promotion`, `promotions.promotion_audit`, `promotions.promotion_variant` |
| Called by | ADM-226, BO-010 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| variants | array of PromotionVariant | yes | (min items 2; max items 4) |
| variants[].id | string (uuid) |  | (read-only) |
| variants[].promotionId | string (uuid) |  | Taken from the path. (read-only) |
| variants[].label | string | yes |  |
| variants[].trafficPercent | integer | yes | Share of traffic. (min 0; max 100) |
| variants[].discountPercent | number |  |  |

**Response**: `object`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| promotionId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| variants | array of PromotionVariant | yes |  |
| variants[].id | string (uuid) |  | (read-only) |
| variants[].promotionId | string (uuid) |  | Taken from the path. (read-only) |
| variants[].label | string | yes |  |
| variants[].trafficPercent | integer | yes | Share of traffic. (min 0; max 100) |
| variants[].discountPercent | number |  |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Set. |
| 400 |  | The variants' trafficPercent values do not sum to 100 (audit R101) |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |


## Group: recommendation

### recommendSeats

**`POST /performances/{performanceId}/seat-recommendations`**: Recommend seats for a party

Best available, best value, closest to stage, or accessible with companions.
Returns contiguous groups where the party requires them — a family of four split across two rows is a complaint, not a booking.
**A guest does this within their own session** (Chinmay, 3 October 2026, Pattern 4; CHG-GCF-003; `x-ticvai-guest-callable`): a guest needs no permission and acts only for themselves, on their own cart, hold, order or payment in their own session. `PRODUCT_VIEW` is what a staff caller must hold to do it for a guest at a till or in the back office (ADR-0025).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `PRODUCT_VIEW` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | core |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Guest callable | True |
| Reads | `catalogue.performance`, `seating.accessible`, `seating.recommendation_rule`, `seating.seat`, `seating.seat_block_item`, `seating.seat_category`, `seating.seat_reservation`, `seating.seat_reservation_item`, `seating.seat_price_band` |
| Writes | - |
| Called by | BO-015, BO-019, BO-1001, BO-1002, BO-1039, GST-049, WEB-007 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| performanceId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**: `SeatRecommendationRequest`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|

| strategy | SeatRecommendationStrategy: enum (bestAvailable, bestValue, closestToStage, accessible, contiguous) | yes |  |
| categoryIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| maxPrice | Money |  | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| maxPrice.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| maxPrice.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| maxPrice.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| accessibleCount | integer |  | Wheelchair spaces in the party. (default 0) |
| maxOptions | integer |  | (max 10; default 3) |

**Response**: `object`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| options | array of SeatRecommendation | yes |  |
| options[].seatIds | array of string | yes |  |
| options[].displayLabels | array of string |  |  |
| options[].totalPrice | Money | yes | On the wire this is three fields; in the database it is one column. |
| options[].totalPrice.amount | string | yes | Decimal string, never a float. (pattern ^-?\d+(\.\d{1,4})?$) |
| options[].totalPrice.currency | string | yes | Resolved from the region, not stored on the row (ADR-0018). (pattern ^[A-Z]{3}$) |
| options[].totalPrice.scale | integer | yes | Resolved from the region alongside currency. (min 0; max 4) |
| options[].categoryId | string (uuid) |  |  |
| options[].isContiguous | boolean | yes |  |
| options[].rank | integer | yes | Best first. |
| options[].rationale | string |  | Why this option was chosen — closest to stage, best value in category, only contiguous block remaining. |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Recommendations, best first |
| 404 |  | No selection satisfies the constraints |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |


## Group: seatMap

### editSeatMapStructure

**`PATCH /seat-maps/{seatMapId}/structure`**: Build a seat map by hand

**The manual seat map building the minutes promised** (MoM 21 Aug 2026; 12 October 2026, CHG-R6-035). BO-954 Venue Canvas and BO-956 Rows & Seats draw sections, rows and seats, but `createSeatMap` names an empty map, `updateSeatMap` saves its name and views and `updateSeats` changes only the category, attribute and status of seats that already exist: a venue with no drawing or manifest to import could not build a map at all. This adds, removes and renumbers the structure of a **draft** map.

**One request, applied in order**: `addSections`, then `addRows` (a row of `seatCount` seats laid out straight or on a curve and numbered from `firstSeatNumber` in `numberingDirection`), then `addSeats` (single seats, for a box or an irregular row), then `removeSeatIds`, then `relabel` (a section code, a row label or a seat number changed). A label already taken in the map, or a removal of a seat on a published map, is refused `409` with the conflicting labels; nothing is applied. **Accepts `Prefer: validate-only`**, as `updateSeats` does, for the AI executor's plan validation (`proposeSeatMapChanges`, kind `numbering`).

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `CAPACITY_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, changes rows of `seating.seat` that another operation creates |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Conflict policy | serverWins |
| Reads | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.seat_map` |
| Writes | `cache:idempotency`, `seating.seat_map`, `seating.section`, `seating.seat` |
| Called by | BO-954, BO-956 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| seatMapId | path | yes | string (uuid) |  |
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |
| Prefer | header |  | enum (validate-only) | Validate, do not write (29 September, AI system design 2.3 and 2.2 D step 4). |

**Request body**

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| addSections | array of object |  |  |
| addSections[].code | string | yes | (max length 20) |
| addSections[].name | string | yes | (max length 100) |
| addSections[].boundary | array of Point |  |  |
| addSections[].boundary[].x | number | yes |  |
| addSections[].boundary[].y | number | yes |  |
| addRows | array of object |  |  |
| addRows[].sectionCode | string | yes |  |
| addRows[].label | string | yes | (max length 10) |
| addRows[].seatCount | integer | yes | (min 1; max 500) |
| addRows[].firstSeatNumber | integer |  | (min 0; default 1) |
| addRows[].numberingDirection | enum (leftToRight, rightToLeft) |  | (default leftToRight) |
| addRows[].start | Point |  |  |
| addRows[].start.x | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].start.y | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].end | Point |  |  |
| addRows[].end.x | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].end.y | number | yes |  |
| addRows[].curveRadius | number |  | Null or absent for a straight row. (nullable) |
| addRows[].categoryId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| addSeats | array of object |  |  |
| addSeats[].sectionCode | string | yes |  |
| addSeats[].rowLabel | string | yes |  |
| addSeats[].seatNumber | string | yes |  |
| addSeats[].position | Point |  |  |
| addSeats[].position.x | number | yes |  |
| addSeats[].position.y | number | yes |  |
| addSeats[].categoryId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| addSeats[].attribute | SeatAttribute: enum (standard, accessible, companion, obstructedView, restrictedLegroom, premium, houseSeat, buffer, …) |  | BL-168. |
| removeSeatIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| relabel | array of object |  |  |
| relabel[].seatId | string (uuid) | yes |  |
| relabel[].sectionCode | string |  |  |
| relabel[].rowLabel | string |  |  |
| relabel[].seatNumber | string |  |  |

**Response**: `object`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| sectionsAdded | integer |  |  |
| seatsAdded | integer |  |  |
| seatsRemoved | integer |  |  |
| seatsRelabelled | integer |  |  |

**Responses**

| Code | Shape | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 200 |  | Applied, or would be applied (validate-only) |
| 409 |  | A label is already taken in the map, or a removal touches a published map |
| 429 | TooManyRequests | Rate limit exceeded for this tenant, venue or principal. |

### setSeatRecommendationRules

**`PUT /seat-recommendation-rules`**: Scoring for best seat, best value, closest, family and accessible

Board 9, and it is the 21 August minute made configurable: *"best-seat ranking (e.g. last-row-is-best vs. first-row-is-best, depending on venue sightlines) must be configurable per seat map/event."*
**Five recommendation kinds, one scoring model.** *Best seat*, *best value*, *closest to stage*, *family together* and *accessible* are the same weighted score with different weights — and writing them as five algorithms guarantees they eventually contradict each other.
`recommendSeats` already exists and consumes this; it had no rules to consume.

|  |  |
|---|---|
| Permission | `CAPACITY_CONFIGURE` |
| Scope level | venue |
| Part of slice | setup, makes `seating.recommendation_rule` non-empty |
| Wave | 1 |
| Offline | no |
| Config scope | venue |
| Reads | `seating.recommendation_rule` |
| Writes | `seating.recommendation_rule` |
| Called by | BO-1034, BO-1035, BO-1036, BO-1037, BO-1038, BO-1041 |

**Parameters**

| Name | In | Required | Type | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Idempotency-Key | header | yes | string (uuid) | Client-generated UUIDv7. |

**Request body**: `SeatRecommendationRules`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| seatMapId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| performanceId | string (uuid) |  | (nullable) |
| profiles | array of object |  |  |
| profiles[].kind | enum (bestSeat, bestValue, closestToStage, familyTogether, accessible, upgrade) |  |  |
| profiles[].weights | object |  | Sightline, distance, centrality, row, price, availability, aisle proximity, legroom. |
| profiles[].preferredSectionIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| profiles[].avoidSectionIds | array of string (uuid) |  |  |
| profiles[].contiguityWeight | number |  | (nullable) |
| reverseRowOrder | boolean |  | Last-row-is-best against first-row-is-best, which the minute names as the worked example and which differs by venue sightline. (default False) |
| explainToGuest | boolean |  | (default True) |
| scopePath | string |  |  |

**Response**: `SeatRecommendationRules`

| Field | Type | Required | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
